- **All five gates passed:** `godot:test` (`GODOT_STUDY_PASS`, including fallback-font glyph checks), `godot:export`, `check`, **104/104 Node tests**, and `godot:browser`. Full progression plus all ten surprises measured **1,728 save bytes** in every browser viewport (limit 4,096). `public/` remains unchanged at **2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567**; browser save v1 remained read-only. ## Historical checkpoint — Landmark discovery bursts, eatery order celebration & Chapter 6 conversation tour (`2026-10-20-landmark-discovery-bursts-and-conversation-tour`)

Builds on `2026-10-20-explore-guide-marker-and-finale` (`719a1c1`). Browser 0.12.0 and `public/` (SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`) are untouched.

- **Landmark & M4 Progress Feel-Good Bursts (`trigger_progress_cue()`, `inspect_tenderloin_museum_hub()`, `inspect_boeddeker_park()`, `inspect_so_what_easter_egg()`, `eatery_counter_interact()`)**:
  - Added `RewardBanner` + 3D `CelebrationBurst` (`+ Vector3.UP * 1.8` clearance above ground/station targets so bursts project into the visible 3D `play_area_rect()` above bottom panels even on `< 600 px` phone screens) + musical chimes for:
    - `"history_comptons"` (`"Living History — %s"`, rose-gold `Color("f5a3d0")`) and `"history_blackhawk"` (`"Jazz History — %s"`, cool-blue `Color("6ec6ff")` with `[329.63, 392.00, 493.88, 587.33]` jazz seventh arpeggio) on first discovery (`read_history_stop()`).
    - `"civic_complete"` (`"Tenant Listening Circle Complete!"`, `Color("d9a2b2")`), `"film_shot"` (`"Film Frame Captured — %s"`, `Color("91bac2")`), and `"film_complete"` (`"Short Film Complete — The Open Seat (3/3)!"`, `Color("ffe066")` with 4-note C-major octave fanfare).
    - `"museum_portal"` (`"Archive Time-Portal — %s"`, `"398 Eddy St (Cadillac Hotel, 1907) · Session era %d/4"`, cyan `Color("7be0e6")`) on the first visit to each of the 4 Archive Time-Portal eras (`"present"`, `"1961_jazz"`, `"1966_comptons"`, `"1907_cadillac"`, tracked in session-only `visited_museum_eras`, with 3D ring/lens reset via `update_museum_portal_3d()`).
    - `"boeddeker_park"` (`"Neighborhood Green — Boeddeker Park"`, mint `Color("7ce8b0")` at `BOEDDEKER_VIEW_TARGET`) on first inspection (`boeddeker_park_visited`).
    - `"so_what_egg"` (`"Creator Easter Egg — 'So What' at 145 Leavenworth & 361 Turk"`, gold `Color("ffd45e")` at `SO_WHAT_VIEW_TARGET`) on first inspection (`so_what_visited`).
    - `"eatery_order"` (`"Order Up: %s"`, gated on `count == 1` in `eatery_counter_interact()` so the first order at each of the 6 fictional sidewalk counters plays a warm 3-note chime and celebration burst while subsequent taps cycle menu items and host lines without spamming bursts).
  - Protected the 4.5-second `"10/10 Surprises:"` grand finale `RewardBanner` in `trigger_feel_good_burst()` (`keep_finale`) so non-finale bursts triggered while `reward_banner_timer > 1.0` do not prematurely overwrite the `10/10` banner.
- **Chapter 6 (`"explore"`) Post-10/10 Conversation Tour (`conversation_actions()`, `run_conversation_action_id()`, `apply_block_panel_mode()`)**:
  - Once all `10/10` neighborhood surprises are discovered in Chapter 6 (`"explore"`), talking with any neighbor in `BlockPanel` offers both `"Visit the Museum"` (`"museum"`, shortened to `"Museum"` on `< 600 px` phone screens) and `"Visit Eateries"` (`"eateries"`, shortened to `"Eateries"` on `< 600 px` phone screens) alongside `"All Block Actions"`, clearing any stale `last_panel_dialogue` `"What's Next?"` prompt when triggered.
- **Independent CLI review & verified fixes**: One successful installed Kibitz native Claude CLI call (`--driver agy --only claude`, exact `claude-opus-5-5`, effort `high`, `rc=0`, `VERDICT: yes-with-fixes`). [Raw report](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-landmark-discovery-bursts-and-conversation-tour/r3/claude.md), [driver anchor](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-landmark-discovery-bursts-and-conversation-tour/r3/driver_anchor.md), [final](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-landmark-discovery-bursts-and-conversation-tour/r3/final.md). All reviewer findings (honest `"Session era %d/4"` wording for session-only `visited_museum_eras`, `BOEDDEKER_VIEW_TARGET` and `SO_WHAT_VIEW_TARGET` camera-centered burst positions, `update_museum_portal_3d()` 3D lens/ring reset, `count == 1` gating on `eatery_order` bursts, `keep_finale` 4.5s banner protection, removal of write-only `eatery_visited`, and narrow-viewport `"Museum"`/`"Eateries"` button labels + `play_area_rect()` burst projection check) were accepted, applied in `godot/main.gd` and `godot/tests/study_test.gd`, and verified.

## Historical checkpoint — Chapter 6 explore GuideMarker/GuideArrow wayfinding, multi-height surprise tap & 10/10 finale (`2026-10-20-explore-guide-marker-and-finale`)

Builds on `2026-10-20-all-neighbor-surprise-keepsakes` (`657efd6`). Browser 0.12.0 and `public/` (SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`) are untouched.

- **Chapter 6 (`"explore"`) 3D Gold `GuideMarker` & Screen-Edge `GuideArrow` Wayfinding for Undiscovered Surprises (`next_explore_surprise_id()`, `current_objective()`, `last_hinted_surprise_id`)**:
  - While fewer than `10/10` neighborhood surprises are discovered in Chapter 6 (`"explore"`), `current_objective()` returns `"has_target": true`, `"target": Vector3(s_pt.x, 0.0, s_pt.y)`, `"target_name": s_title`, and `"surprise_id": next_s_id` so the 3D floating gold diamond (`GuideMarker`) and off-screen edge arrow (`GuideArrow`, with narrow-viewport label truncation and `play_area_rect()` clamping) guide the player to the next undiscovered surprise.
  - `next_explore_surprise_id()` prioritizes the open character panel's undiscovered paired surprises (`NEIGHBOR_SURPRISE_PAIRS` for `Bea`, `Celeste`, `Rook`, `Dez`, or `Thanh`), then `last_hinted_surprise_id` (set when a neighbor answers `"What's Next?"` in `"explore"` mode so the marker stays locked onto their hinted surprise even after closing the panel with `Goodbye (Esc)`), and then the first undiscovered entry in `NEIGHBORHOOD_SURPRISE_IDS`.
  - Once all `10/10` surprises are discovered, `current_objective()` celebrates `"All 10/10 surprises & keepsakes found! Try Museum (Shift+M), Eateries (Shift+E), Evening (Shift+L)"` with `"has_target": false`, hiding `GuideMarker` and `GuideArrow`.
- **Single-Resolver `Shift+D` & `"Spot Surprise"` Agreement (`cycle_neighborhood_surprise()`, `run_conversation_action_id("surprise")`)**:
  - In Chapter 6 (`"explore"`), both `Shift+D` (`cycle_neighborhood_surprise()`) and the `"Spot Surprise"` conversation button resolve through `current_objective().surprise_id`, guaranteeing that `Shift+D` and `"Spot Surprise"` always inspect the exact surprise indicated by the gold `GuideMarker`, `GuideArrow`, and `ObjectiveLabel`.
- **Multi-Height 3D Scene Tap Hitbox for Surprise Vignettes, Labels & Active `GuideMarker` (`surprise_screen_distance()`, `surprise_has_guide_marker()`, `GUIDE_MARKER_LIFT_LOW`)**:
  - `surprise_screen_distance()` samples heights `[0.8, 1.6, 2.6]` (`surp_world_d <= 5.5`) for all surprise vignettes/labels and additionally samples `GUIDE_MARKER_LIFT_LOW` (`4.6 m`) and the live `GuideMarker` gem center (`surp_world_d <= 9.5`) for the surprise currently bearing the floating `GuideMarker`, so tapping a surprise vignette, its `SurpriseLabel`, or its floating gold `GuideMarker` in street view reliably triggers `inspect_neighborhood_surprise(s_id)` without shadowing nearby eatery or museum targets.
- **10/10 All Keepsakes Grand Finale Celebration (`inspect_neighborhood_surprise()`, `trigger_progress_cue("surprise_finale")`)**:
  - Discovering the 10th neighborhood surprise triggers an explicit `"surprise_finale"` 4-note ascending C-major octave fanfare (`[523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.50]`, `cue_arpeggio_duration = 0.68`, `cue_chime_freq = 1046.50`) and a 4.5-second `RewardBanner` (`"10/10 Surprises: %s"` / `"All 10/10 Keepsakes! · Sparks: %d · Keepsake: %s"`).
- **Independent CLI review & verified fixes**: One successful installed Kibitz native Claude CLI call (`--driver agy --only claude`, exact `claude-opus-5-5`, effort `high`, `rc=0`, `VERDICT: yes-with-fixes`). [Raw report](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-explore-guide-marker-and-finale/r3/claude.md), [driver anchor](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-explore-guide-marker-and-finale/r3/driver_anchor.md), [final](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-explore-guide-marker-and-finale/r3/final.md). All reviewer findings (unifying `cycle_neighborhood_surprise()` with `current_objective().surprise_id`, scoping `GUIDE_MARKER_LIFT_LOW` tap sampling to the marker-bearing surprise, preserving `last_hinted_surprise_id` when opening a neighbor panel whose own pairs are already found, clamping `GuideArrow` label inside `play_area_rect()`, and explicit `"surprise_finale"` cue dispatch) were accepted, applied in `godot/main.gd` and `godot/tests/study_test.gd`, and verified.

## Historical checkpoint — All 10 neighbor 3D surprise keepsakes & personalized explore hints (`2026-10-20-all-neighbor-surprise-keepsakes`)

Builds on `2026-10-20-character-panels-dialogue-parity` (`eb529a6`). Browser 0.12.0 and `public/` (SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`) are untouched.

  - **Bea (`neighbor_nodes[0]/NeighborProp`)**:
    - `"mayor_pomp_event"` -> `MayorRibbonRosette` (`bea_mayor_ribbon_node`: crimson-and-gold ceremonial ribbon rosette with gold seal and two ribbon tails pinned to Bea's rollator walker bar).
    - `"good_donut_and_bagel"` -> `BakeryTreatBox` (`bea_treat_box_node`: pastel pink bakery box holding a miniature pink-frosted donut and toasted everything bagel on Bea's rollator walker).

    - `"hidden_mural_145"` -> `HiddenMuralSketchCard` (`hidden_mural_sketch_node` on the right easel leg).

    - `"jeffrey_record"` -> `JeffreyRecordTurntable` (`jeffrey_turntable_node` atop `RookSpeaker`).

  - **Thanh (`neighbor_nodes[4]/NeighborProp`)**:
    - `"impromptu_drag_show"` -> `StarlightRunwayCrown` (`thanh_starlight_fan_node` on Thanh's cedar tea crate: rose-magenta & teal pleated hand fan and gold star tiara).
    - `"bite_size_nachos"` -> `RicoMiniNachoBag` (`thanh_nacho_bag_node` beside Thanh's thermos on her cedar tea crate: orange-and-gold bite-sized nacho bag, golden tortilla chip, and salsa verde cup).
- **Keepsake Payoff Feedback, Immediate Panel Reflection & Personalized Explore Hints (`inspect_neighborhood_surprise()`, `ask_whats_next()`, `active_panel_neighbor_idx()`)**:
  - `inspect_neighborhood_surprise(s_id)` formats `RewardBanner` body as `"%d/%d Surprises · Sparks: %d · Keepsake: %s"` (with 2-line word wrap on `reward_body_label`), surfaces `SURPRISE_KEEPSAKE_META[s_id].label` in `info.text` and `announce_a11y_status()`, and when `newly_discovered` is true either immediately displays the paired neighbor's newly unlocked surprise reflection in their open panel or clears stale `last_panel_dialogue` hints without wiping dialogue on revisits.
  - `ask_whats_next()` in `"explore"` mode reuses `next_undiscovered_surprise_for_neighbor(speaker)` to give a personal first-person direction from the active neighbor (`Bea`, `Celeste`, `Rook`, `Dez`, or `Thanh`) to their first undiscovered paired surprise (with `compass_phrase()` direction and street name), or to the next undiscovered block surprise once both of their paired surprises are found, or celebrates unlocking all 10/10 keepsakes when complete.
  - `get_a11y_status_snapshot()` reports `"keepsakes_visible": visible_surprise_keepsake_count()`.
- **Independent CLI review & verified fixes**: One successful installed Kibitz native Claude CLI call (`--driver agy --only claude`, exact `claude-opus-5-5`, effort `high`, `rc=0`, `VERDICT: yes-with-fixes`). [Raw report](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-all-neighbor-surprise-keepsakes/r3/claude.md), [driver anchor](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-all-neighbor-surprise-keepsakes/r3/driver_anchor.md), [final](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-all-neighbor-surprise-keepsakes/r3/final.md). All reviewer findings (clearing stale fallback hints when a newly found surprise belongs to another neighbor, shared `active_panel_neighbor_idx()` / `block_panel_neighbor_idx()` speaker resolution, `apply_save_envelope()` `last_panel_dialogue` reset, `announce_a11y_status()` keepsake suffix, `next_undiscovered_surprise_for_neighbor()` reuse in `ask_whats_next()`, and `MuralPanel`-open discovery test coverage) were accepted, applied in `godot/main.gd` and `godot/tests/study_test.gd`, and verified.

## Historical checkpoint — Neighborhood surprises, Character Talk UI & feel-good reward bursts (`2026-10-20-story-surprises-character-ui`)

Builds on the 2026-10-19 Claude Code 3D, LiDAR, street-tree, and conversation-first pass (`8827618`). Browser 0.12.0 and `public/` (SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`) are untouched.

- **10 Interactive 3D Neighborhood Surprises (`godot/main.gd`)**:
  - Built under `NeighborhoodSurprises` (`build_neighborhood_surprises()`, `surprise_sidewalk_site()` verified outside all 188 footprints with mutual separation, cycled via **`Shift+D`** or the `Surprise 10` button in `BlockPanel`, or discovered by walking within `4.2 m` / tapping in street view). Every surprise is explicitly framed as a fictional neighborhood story or whimsical moment set on real measured Tenderloin streets:
    1. `jeffrey_record` — **Jeffrey's Lost 1961 Record** beside the `361 Turk St` `'So What'` awning (`Vector3(-376.2, 0.0, 62.4)`): blue-and-gold sleeve and spinning 33 RPM vinyl LP with a warm note to bring it to Rook's speaker.
    2. `hidden_mural_145` — **The Hidden Mural at 145 Leavenworth** below the 8th-floor bay window (`Vector3(-284.5, 0.0, 95.8)`): golden rooftop hound, twilight saxophone, and a chalk arrow pointing up to the 8th-floor window.
    3. `impromptu_drag_show` — **Surprise Impromptu Drag Show** near `Taylor & Turk` in the Transgender Cultural District (`Vector3(-8.5, 0.0, -18.5)`): rose-magenta sidewalk runway carpet, footlights, and `Starlight Vee` performing an evening lipsync number.
    4. `viking_dirt_biker` — **Random Guy on a Dirt Bike in a Viking Helmet** on the `200 block of Turk St` (`Vector3(-218.0, 0.0, 39.2)`): `Knobby-Tire Dirt Bike` with gently rocking wheel bounce (`viking_bike_mesh`) and rider in a horned `VikingHelmet` waving hello.
    5. `cutesy_dino` — **Small Cutesy Sidewalk Dinosaur (`Pip the Tiny Dino`)** by Boeddeker Park (`Vector3(-148.0, 0.0, -52.0)`): mint-green friendly sidewalk dinosaur with pastel back plates, rosy cheeks, tiny arms, and a wagging tail (`cutesy_dino_tail_node`).
    6. `magic_carpet_chef` — **Wild Woman Chef on a Magic Carpet (`Chef Zafira`)** on the `Eddy & Leavenworth` corridor (`Vector3(-258.0, 0.0, -34.0)`): woven burgundy-and-gold fringed rug hovering and bobbing above the sidewalk (`magic_carpet_chef_node`), with `Chef Zafira` in a tall puff toque (`ChefToquePuff`) tossing a sizzling copper skillet of garlic noodles.
    7. `bite_size_nachos` — **Bite-Sized Bags of Sidewalk Nachos (`Nacho Captain Rico`)** on `Turk & Taylor` (`Vector3(-64.0, 0.0, 18.0)`): cheerful fiesta cart stocked with warm bite-sized bags of tortilla chips, golden queso cups, and pickled jalapeños.
    8. `good_donut_and_bagel` — **A Really Good Donut & Everything Bagel (`Baker Mae`)** on `Eddy & Taylor` (`Vector3(18.0, 0.0, -56.0)`): brass pastry rack with a warm pink-frosted glaze donut and a toasted sesame-poppy everything bagel with cream cheese.
    9. `clown_horn_walker` — **Guy Walking Around with a Clown Horn (`Honker Barnaby`)** on `Jones & Turk` (`Vector3(-124.0, 0.0, 18.0)`): strolling neighbor with a brass-and-red-bulb squeeze horn (`clown_horn_bulb_mesh` pulsing when honked) who plays a cheerful two-note *HONK-HONK!* for the block.
    10. `mayor_pomp_event` — **Impromptu 'Mayor Public Event' & Ribbon Cutting** on `Eddy & Leavenworth` (`Vector3(-296.0, 0.0, -52.0)`): miniature red carpet, velvet rope stanchions, civic seal podium, `The Block Mayor` in a sash, `FanfareBugler` with a brass herald trumpet, `GiantGoldScissors` snipping a satin ribbon, and a 4-note *Pomp and Circumstance* ceremonial chime.
  - **Persistence**: `discovered_surprises` is persisted in `user://godot_block_save_v1.json` and Web `localStorage['tenderloin-redux.godot.save.v1']` (additive array validated against `NEIGHBORHOOD_SURPRISE_IDS`; full M1–M4 + 10-surprises save envelope stays well within the `<= 4096` byte budget). Re-inspecting an already-discovered surprise frames the vignette and speech bubble without re-writing save state or re-firing the celebration burst.
- **Character Talk UI (`apply_character_panel_theme()`, `NEIGHBOR_STORY_BADGES`, `spark_count()`)**:
  - Talking with any neighbor tints the active panel border (`MuralPanel`, `MusicPanel`, or `BlockPanel`) in that character's coat accent color (`StyleBoxFlat` cached per neighbor ID), displays their story role badge (`Block Organizer`, `Muralist & Colorist`, `Sound Engineer & guitarist`, `Poet & Rhythm Host`, `Tea & Herb Gardener`), shows a wrapping conversation turn indicator (`Turn 1/5` .. `Turn 5/5` in lockstep with the 5-turn dialogue arrays), and surfaces the live `Sparks: N` neighborhood progress counter (`spark_count()`, strictly invariant across save/reload).
- **Feel-Good Engagement & Reward Bursts (`RewardBanner`, `CelebrationBurst`, `trigger_progress_cue()`)**:
  - First-time progress milestones (meeting a neighbor, first chat with Celeste, placing each mural motif, completing the 3/3 mural, completing Rook's soundcheck, opening the block party, visiting real history stops, discovering each of the 10 neighborhood surprises, and completing M1–M4 chapters) trigger a top-center gold `RewardBanner` toast, a 3D rising `CelebrationBurst` (`SparkGem_0..7` + `CelebrationBurstLabel`), and a 3-note rising major-triad or *Pomp and Circumstance* ceremonial chime in `advance_music_clock()`.
- **Independent CLI review & verified fixes**: One successful installed Kibitz native Claude CLI call (`--driver agy --only claude`, exact `claude-opus-5-5`, effort `high`, `rc=0`, `VERDICT: yes-with-fixes`). [Raw report](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-story-surprises-character-ui/r3/claude.md), [scope](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-story-surprises-character-ui/r3/scope_receipt.md), [driver anchor](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-story-surprises-character-ui/r3/driver_anchor.md), [judgment](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-story-surprises-character-ui/r3/judgment.md), [final](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-20-story-surprises-character-ui/r3/final.md). All reviewer findings were accepted, applied in `godot/main.gd` and `godot/tests/study_test.gd`, and verified.

## Historical checkpoint — Godot 3D improvement pass (2026-10-19, Claude Code)

Builds on the two un-logged façade commits that followed Round 9 (`7c47753` projecting bay windows, awnings, curbs, Turk bike lane, crosswalks; `4d5f16f` Street-View-referenced block palettes, 3D fire escapes, ADA curb ramps, and the mobile Field Photo Guide). Browser 0.12.0 and `public/` (SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`) are untouched.

- **Round A — rooftops & landmarks (`godot/main.gd`)**:
  - **Rooftop details** (`build_rooftop_details()`): each building gets a separate `RooftopDetails` child mesh, so building meshes keep their measured LiDAR AABB height and the details hide during camera cutaway. Coped parapet walls on every roof edge ≥ 6 m, elevator penthouses with doors (≥ 18 m), cedar water tanks with hoops and conical lids on timber trestles (≥ 14 m), gabled glass skylights, brick chimneys and HVAC units. Placement is deterministic per `sf16_bldgid` and every item is verified inside its own footprint (`find_roof_spot()`, edge clearance, no overlaps). Current counts: 179 parapets, 29 water tanks, 43 penthouses, 9 corner turrets.
  - **SF corner bay turrets with cupolas** (`compute_corner_tower_sites()`, `add_corner_tower()`): the tallest (≥ 15 m) building corner within 15 m of each of the 10 real OSM street intersections, plus an occasional second tall corner, gets an octagonal turret with per-story windows, belt courses, a cornice ring, a patina/slate/terracotta dome, and a gold finial.
  - **Muni trolleybus overhead** (`build_muni_overhead_lines()`): a contact-wire pair above the Turk and Eddy travel lanes (31 Balboa corridor), with span wires and hangers between sidewalk poles every ~30 m; 98 poles, all on Turk/Eddy sidewalks outside every footprint and clear of intersections.
  - **Boeddeker Park** (`build_boeddeker_park()`, `inspect_boeddeker_park()`): the real outline of OpenStreetMap way `33619301` (240 Eddy St, NE corner of Eddy & Jones; raw response in `godot/data/source/boeddeker_park.osm.json`) as a lawn with a picket fence, Eddy St gate arch, winding path, benches, a play structure, and 24 shade trees whose `Canopy` pivots are ready for wind sway. Tap the gate label to frame it. Trees, path, and furnishings are stylized, not surveyed.
  - **Tests**: `godot/tests/study_test.gd` adds roof-item-inside-footprint, turret-at-real-intersection, cutaway-hides-details / overview-restores-details, trolley-pole sidewalk placement, park-tree placement, park outline orientation, and park inspect/tap checks.
- **Round B — living streetscape & overhead speech bubbles (`godot/main.gd`)**:
  - **3D speech bubbles** (`show_speech_bubble()`, `show_neighbor_speech()`): one shared billboard `Label3D` bubble with a chamfered cream background, border, and tail pops above whoever is talking: Bea, Dez, and Thanh (`talk_with_neighbor()` and arrival greetings), Celeste (`talk_with_celeste()`), Rook (`check_sound_with_rook()`), the museum docent (`inspect_tenderloin_museum_hub()`, one line per Time-Portal era), and eatery hosts. It is rescaled from the orthographic camera size every frame so it keeps a readable on-screen size at any zoom, slides sideways to stay inside the viewport, and closes after its reading time, when the view moves more than 45 m away, or on overview.
  - **Day / evening** (`toggle_evening_lights()`, **`Shift+L`**; plain `L` still opens the wide overview): evening lowers the sun and ambient light and makes **194 additive light pools** glow on the sidewalks, from street-facing ground-floor storefront windows (`build_light_pools()`, colored to match each storefront's warm or cyan glass) and under the globe street lamps. By day the pools stay faint.
  - **Swaying trees** (`update_tree_sway()`): the street trees (`build_street_trees()`, one MultiMesh per part; since replaced by the real DataSF trees, see below), the 24 Boeddeker Park trees, and the existing planters all sway gently with a slow gust cycle.
  - **Interactive eatery counters** (`build_eatery_counters()`, `eatery_counter_interact()`): visiting an eatery with `Shift+E` opens a sidewalk counter between curb and storefront, with stools, a menu board, a host, and signature items (steaming pho bowl, naan basket and lassi, a spinning record on a tube-amp turntable, banh mi with iced coffee, draft and billiard balls under a flickering neon sign, an olallieberry pie under glass). The host greets you in a speech bubble. Tapping the counter orders the next house item and the host answers. The framed counter and museum hub now feed the camera cutaway, so occluding buildings turn translucent.
  - **Web font fix**: 22 labels and info lines used glyphs missing from Godot's Web default font (Open Sans SemiBold) and rendered as hex boxes: `★`, `═`, `║`, `♪`, `→`. They now use `«` `»` `|` `~`. A new test scans every non-ASCII character in `godot/main.gd` and every live `Label3D`/`Label`/`Button` against `ThemeDB.fallback_font.has_char()`.
  - **Fix**: a pre-existing street lamp at `(96, 4.2)` stood inside footprint `201006.0004056`. Hand-placed lamps and planters that fall inside a footprint are now snapped onto the sidewalk (`sidewalk_safe_point()`).
- **Conversation-first UX (`godot/main.gd`)**, after creator feedback that talking to characters matters as much as the 3D:
  - **Next step** (`current_objective()`, header `ObjectiveLabel`): one plain-language goal derived from saved progress. Before opening: meet the nearest neighbor you haven't met, paint the 3 motifs, soundcheck with Rook, open the party with Bea. After opening: photo, gear returns (Y, each item pointing to its owner), the schedule and hosting (Z), the listening circle (Q), the film (.), then the real history stops (J, H) and free exploring.
  - **Gold guide marker** (`GuideMarker`): a screen-constant diamond above the next target. It hides when you're beside the target or already talking with them.
  - **Talk prompt** (`TalkPrompt`): "Talk to Bea · T or tap" above the nearest neighbor within 6 m. **T** now talks to whoever is nearby (walking over if needed) instead of always heading to Celeste.
  - **Conversation mode** in BlockPanel (`show_conversation()`): talking with Bea, Dez, or Thanh shows their line (2 lines, not clipped) with **Keep Chatting (T) · What's Next? · Goodbye (Esc)** and three context actions: the next objective (e.g. "Go Meet Celeste", "Open Block Party!"), another unmet neighbor or a real history stop, and **All Block Actions** for the full 13-button hub (still on `G`). **What's Next?** has the neighbor give the next step with an honest direction (`compass_phrase()`: metres under 100 m, else ~145 m blocks; +x east, +z south). **Esc** says goodbye when you're standing still.
  - **Off-screen guide arrow** (`GuideArrow`): when the next target is off-screen, a gold arrow at the edge of the 3D view points to it with its name and distance (e.g. "Celeste · 53 m").
  - **Action list guidance**: the full 13-button hub highlights the suggested next step in gold and dims steps that unlock later. Dimmed steps stay clickable and explain what to do first. Celeste's and Rook's panels now end with the same **Goodbye (Esc)**.
  - **What's Next? everywhere**: on the bottom bar (it replaces the developer-only "« Prev Building"; "Next Building »" still wraps through all 58) and in Celeste's and Rook's panels.
  - **Phones / high-DPI (fix)**: the Web canvas renders at device pixels, so a 2× phone got the desktop layout with 25-CSS-px buttons and about 9-px text (worse on 3× phones). `apply_device_pixel_ratio()` now sets the root window's `content_scale_factor` to `devicePixelRatio` on the Web, so the UI lays out in CSS pixels while 3D renders at full resolution. Verified in 390×667 @2× emulation. On narrow screens during a conversation, the title shortens to "TENDERLOIN REDUX", the header info line steps aside (the panel and bubble carry the words), labels shorten ("Chat More", "Meet Celeste", "All Actions"), and the edge arrow hides while someone is speaking.
  - **Conversation framing**: `frame_conversation()` pans the camera so the speaker and their bubble sit in the visible 3D area above the open panel. Goodbye returns the camera to following you.
  - **Deeper conversations** (`neighbor_lines()`, `celeste_dialogue_text()`, `rook_dialogue_text()`): five turns per neighbor before and after opening (up from three), so Keep Chatting doesn't repeat right away. Old button names were updated to the new labels, and developer phrasing ("2010 wall", "its own Godot save", wall distances) became natural speech. New lines describe game mechanics or facts already in the data: the Compton's plaque at Turk & Taylor, the Black Hawk at Turk & Hyde (1949–63), and Boeddeker Park's direction from Bea, computed from the OSM outline as "about a block northwest".
  - **Plain-language header**: walking shows "On Turk Street, near Taylor Street" (`location_phrase()`, from the real OSM intersections) instead of coordinates and footprint IDs. Arrival lines drop the "m from 201006.…" readouts. The first-run header explains talking and walking in plain words. Tests cover objectives, marker, prompt, T, dialogue choices, the hub switch, Esc, and 44 px / no-overlap layout at 1100×760, 390×667, and 320×667.
- **Story round: chapters + the Long Table / Open Floor choice (from `docs/CONCEPT.md`)**:
  - **Chapters** (`current_chapter()`, `STORY_CHAPTERS`): 1 Get the Block Ready, 2 The Gathering, 3 Another Afternoon, 4 A Listening Hour, 5 The Open Seat, 6 Your Block. The chapter comes from the furthest progress reached, and the header reads "Chapter N: Title · Next: …". A **story card** (`StoryCard`) introduces the current chapter at startup and at each chapter change. Chapter 1 sets up the premise (you live on the block and help co-host a gathering your neighbors already started) and says plainly that the gathering and neighbors are fictional while the streets and history are real. Continue, Esc, any key, or a tap closes it, and it never lets a tap walk you.
  - **The bounded choice** (`choose_gathering_layout()`): the 3D track couldn't make the concept's central choice before; it only imported it from browser saves. After you meet everyone, the next step is to choose **Bea's Long Table** (conversation) or **Dez & Celeste's Open Floor** (art and music) in conversation. Neighbors pitch their own idea in the first person. The choice builds visible 3D seating in a fictional curb-lane closure on the north side of Turk beside Bea (`GatheringLayouts`: barricades plus a long table with 16 stools and Thanh's tea, or a painted open floor with a riser, mic, and lettering panel). The sidewalk route and crosswalks stay clear. String lights come on when the gathering opens. You can change your mind before opening. The other idea becomes a "next time" promise that neighbors remember afterward (`layout_memory_line()`), and the keepsake is titled "Stories around the Long Table" or "A little room on the Open Floor".
  - **Saves**: a new optional `gathering_layout` field. Old or opened saves without it fall back to the keepsake layout or the Long Table, and an invalid value is dropped without touching other progress. Browser v1 save import carries the browser layout. Opening without choosing defaults to Bea's Long Table, so existing flows and tests are unchanged.
- **Night windows** (`BUILDING_SHADER_CODE`, `building_material()`, `glass_flag()`): every building and rooftop mesh shares one vertex-color spatial shader that matches the old StandardMaterial3D by day. Window glass carries a lit/unlit flag in vertex alpha. In evening mode (Shift+L), about 55% of upper-story windows, every storefront, and most turret windows glow, while unlit glass darkens. The pattern is deterministic per building; it's an atmosphere effect, not a claim about real occupancy.
- **Accuracy check**: the Turk/Eddy overhead wires match real 31 Balboa trolleybus service (inbound Eddy, outbound Turk between Larkin and Mason; SFMTA, Wikipedia). Sources were added to `godot/data/SOURCES.md`. No Muni rail or streetcars are modeled, and no buses were added.
- **Real street trees + eatery name check (accuracy)**:
  - **Street trees from the city inventory**: the rule-placed trees (every 22 m along each curb) are replaced by the **371 real trees** from the San Francisco Street Tree Inventory (DataSF `tkzw-k3nq`, PDDL 1.0) inside the modeled area, each at its recorded position. `scripts/build-street-trees.mjs` (`npm run godot:trees`) derives `godot/data/street_trees.json` offline from the retained raw response (498 records). It drops 9 empty "Potential Site" basins, 79 records outside the modeled area, and 39 that fall inside a 2010 footprint (18 inside `201006.0004056`, an open footprint question). `load_street_tree_data()` sizes each canopy from its trunk diameter and tints it by species (London plane 132, Brisbane box 75, New Zealand Christmas tree 16, Indian laurel fig 10, ash, ornamental pear; others get neutral greens). The schematic trolley poles skip stations within 1.5 m of a real tree. The tree file adds about 58 KB; the Web `index.pck` is 1.42 MB.
  - **Eateries**: the six eateries stay fictional, and are now labeled "(fictional)" on their 3D signs and in the Shift+E guide line. Their names were checked against real San Francisco businesses. "Golden Lotus Pho & Noodle House" (there's a real Golden Lotus at 631 Larkin St) is now **Bowl & Basil Pho Counter**. "Midnight Sun Diner" (a real Castro bar) is now **Open Seat Diner & Pie Counter**. "The Brass Owl Neon Tavern" (close to The Owl Tree at 601 Post St) is now **Copper Cue Tavern & Billiards**, and its neon owl became a neon cue ball. The made-up "4.8/5"-style star ratings are gone everywhere.

  - **Gates**: `GODOT_STUDY_PASS`, `npm run check`, `npm test` **103/103**, `npm run godot:export`, and `npm run godot:browser` passing at 1100×760, 390×667, and 320×667.
- **LiDAR roofs for all 188 footprints, and every current building the 2010 layer lacks**:
  - **Coverage**: the pipeline now covers the whole modeled area: 650 LiDAR octree nodes (149 MB), 31 M points, a wider OSM building query, and Roofer pass 1 on 188 + 10 outlines in 50 s. Pass 2 retries 3 footprints the full run flagged as having insufficient points (an isolated run reconstructs them within 0.4 m of 2010) and measures the OSM outlines of rebuilt sites. Retained sources were renamed (`roofer_lod13.city.jsonl`, `roofer_lod13_pass2.city.jsonl`, `osm_buildings.json`, `lidar_ept_manifest.json`). Opening-block results are unchanged from the first run (same parts and areas).
  - **Heights from the city ground**: Roofer's ground estimate sits up to 5.5 m low on sloped blocks, which inflated heights and created spurious raised parts. LiDAR heights are now measured from the city's surveyed median ground: 80% of standing footprints agree with 2010 within 0.56 m (was 1.40 m), and raised parts went from 177 to **125**.
  - **Current buildings (8)**. Missing from 2010: Livat Mall, **TL Residences at 361 Turk St and 145 Leavenworth St** (8 storeys each, 25–26 m), 180 Jones St (9 storeys), and an unnamed building. Rebuilt: Serif, **Prism** (1028 Market St, 13 storeys, 38.6 m on a 9.7 m 2010 footprint), and 993 Market St (one storey on an 11.8 m 2010 footprint). Five 2010 footprints are superseded. The rule is "contains ≥ 70% of a 2010 footprint whose LiDAR height changed by more than 5 m", so outlines whose centroid lies outside the 2010 query window, like Prism, still count. About 866 m² of LiDAR building area, mostly a ~5 m structure north of Turk between Leavenworth and Hyde, has no OSM outline and stays unmodeled.
  - **Measured roofs everywhere**: no rule-placed rooftop items or stylized turrets remain (turrets 6 → 0). Every roof shows only parapets, measured raised parts, and creator survey items.

The "So What" 8th-floor window sits on its real 8th floor (LiDAR height split over its 8 OSM storeys) on the Leavenworth facade, and the 361 Turk awning is on the other TL Residences building's Turk frontage. The TL Residences terracotta/buff palette now colors those two buildings instead of five unrelated 2010 footprints that a coordinate box had painted.

Its neighbors, identified by OSM name and address, are the Kelly Cullen Community (220 Golden Gate Ave, former YMCA: peach-tan brick, cream cornice, fire escape) and the Page Hotel (161 Leavenworth: tan brick over a slate blue-green ground floor). `CREATOR_PHOTO_PALETTES` holds the colors; the photo is not copied. A scene test checks the three palettes.

The canopy stays at the entrance under the sign. A rooftop dog park with turf, planters, benches, and a fire pit that glows in evening mode sits on TL Residences' measured top roof. Across Leavenworth, the Compass Children's Center rooftop playground (OSM childcare node on 140–156 Leavenworth) has green turf, a mesh (net) canopy on posts, and small play pieces. Positions come from the measured outlines; the layout is stylized. Tests check the window's bay and row and that both rooftops sit on their own roofs.

It checks pass-2 coverage, city-ground heights, both TL Residences buildings, 180 Jones, Prism, 993 Market, and every superseded footprint's evidence. The scene test checks all 188 roofs measured, no rule-placed items, 8 additions, 5 superseded, and the easter egg on the real facades and 8th floor.
- **2023 LiDAR roofs and buildings the 2010 layer lacks**, after creator feedback that the 2010 footprints miss buildings:
  - **Source and tooling**: 2023 USGS 3DEP San Francisco LiDAR (`CA_SanFrancisco_1_B23`, flown 2023-04-20, public domain), fetched as only the 287 Entwine octree nodes over the opening block (65.7 MB, not committed; hashes in `godot/data/source/lidar_ept_opening_manifest.json`). Points are cropped and reprojected into the game frame (`scripts/lidar/`), and the official Roofer 1.0.0 Windows build (GPL-3.0, not bundled) reconstructs LoD 1.3 roofs in about 15 s. `scripts/build-lidar-2023.mjs` (`npm run godot:lidar`) derives `godot/data/lidar_2023.json` (70 KB) from the retained Roofer output, OSM response, and `buildings.json`.
  - **Check against 2010**: LiDAR ground matches the city's NAVD88 ground within ±1.7 m, and median roofs match `height_m` within about 1 m for most of the 58 footprints. 16% of the LiDAR building area in the opening window had no 2010 footprint. All of it lies in two current OSM buildings: **Livat Mall** (945 Market St, no 2010 footprint, 28.2 m) and **Serif** (960 Market St, 37.7 m by LiDAR vs 36.6 m in OSM). Serif replaced three ~10 m 2010 footprints (`201006.0004056`, `0005472`, `0010048`).
  - **In the scene**: the two buildings stand on their OSM outlines at LiDAR height in a `CurrentBuildings` node and block walking. The three replaced footprints are hidden and leave physics and collision, but keep their records, so `all_buildings` stays 188 and Next Building still cycles 58; selecting one says "Replaced: … Serif (960 Market Street)". Collision and walking queries (`building_containing_point()`, `min_wall_distance()`, `nearest_building_info()`) use the new `current_buildings` set. Every opening-block roof gets its measured raised parts (61 at the time: penthouses, upper wings, a round corner tower about 90 m south of Turk & Taylor, towers), with window rows per storey that glow in evening mode. The rule-placed tanks, penthouses, skylights, vents, and stylized corner turrets are no longer drawn on measured roofs (turrets: 9 → 6, all on unmeasured wider-area roofs). Building bases keep the 2010 median, so every AABB still equals `height_m`. The selection info line compares 2010 with 2023.
  - **Knock-on accuracy fixes**: Thanh stood 1.0 m from Serif's real wall and moved 1.8 m to (11.10, 24.97), now 2.8 m clear. Street trees went from 371 to **345**. Dropped: 50 records stacked on shared coordinates (address placeholders; 16 on one point inside Serif were the earlier "18 trees inside `0004056`") and the older record of 9 duplicate pairs. Four Market St London planes recorded just inside Livat's outline moved about 4 m out to their LiDAR canopies (`moved_m` recorded). Rook's speaker test now expects Serif as the nearest standing building.
  - **Tests**: `tests/godot-data.test.mjs` re-derives `lidar_2023.json` and checks the retained-source hashes, that Roofer ran on exactly the 2010 + candidate footprints, ground and height agreement, that raised parts stay inside their own footprint, that both additions and all three superseded footprints are what the evidence says, and the tree rules (no stacked coordinates, moves recorded). `godot/tests/study_test.gd` checks LiDAR roof coverage, that no rule-placed items remain on measured roofs, that replaced footprints are hidden and out of physics and collision, that additions are solid at LiDAR height, and the replacement info line. `index.pck` is 1.49 MB.
- **Round C — Field Guide survey importer (`scripts/apply-field-survey.mjs`, `npm run field:apply`)**:
  - Reads a `tenderloin-field-survey-*.json` export (schema `tenderloin-redux.field-survey.v1`; with no path it takes the newest one in `tools/field-guide/`) and derives per-`sf16_bldgid` overrides. Stop tags reach the nearest ≤ 4 footprints within 28 m of the stop (`--radius`, `--per-stop`). Building-inspector tags and facade/trim colors apply to that footprint and win on conflict. Unknown ids, unknown or street-level tags, and invalid colors are reported and skipped. Photos and notes are never copied.
  - Dry run by default. `--apply` rewrites only the generated `FIELD_SURVEY_OVERRIDES` block between markers in `godot/main.gd` (idempotent). `--clear` restores the empty block, and `--out` writes a JSON report.
  - `godot/main.gd` interprets the block through `FIELD_SURVEY_TAG_STYLES` / `field_survey_style()`: brick, stucco, and paint tags recolor the facade, terracotta trim recolors the cornice, and murals tint the ground floor. Bay-window, awning, flat-marquee, fire-escape, and mezzanine tags force those facade elements; rooftop water tank, penthouse, and skylight tags force rooftop details; the neon tag adds a vertical blade sign on the longest street-facing facade. Measured LiDAR heights are untouched. Selecting a surveyed building shows its survey tags.
  - **Field Guide (`tools/field-guide/index.html`)**: the building inspector now has per-building tag toggles and facade/trim color pickers, and the stop tags include rooftop tags. **Fix**: the guide's GPS origin (37.783135, -122.4112237) was about 39 m west of the game's Turk & Taylor origin (37.7832203, -122.410784), which offset the GPS dot and Street View links. It now uses `buildings.json`'s `origin`.
  - Verified end-to-end: building tags, a color, and a stop tag set in the live Field Guide UI, exported (Blob captured in-page, no download), imported (4 overrides), applied, `GODOT_STUDY_PASS`, then `--clear` back to the shipped empty block.

## Historical checkpoint — Godot Round 9: M6 release quality, original SVG builders/export parity, measured local performance, visual street life (Dodge Alley Concert & 145 Leavenworth / 361 Turk 'So What' Easter Egg) & provenance verification (`2026-10-19-godot-m6-release-quality`)

Separate `godot/` Godot 4.7.2 standard/GDScript Compatibility project now implements **Round 9 of `docs/GODOT_APPROACH.md`**: completing **M6 Release Quality, Original SVG Generation/Export Parity, Measured Local Performance, Visual Character & Street-Level Life, and Complete Provenance Inventory** across the Godot and browser tracks. Browser 0.12.0 remains unchanged and playable at `http://127.0.0.1:4173/` (`public/` SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`); Godot Web preview runs at `http://127.0.0.1:4174/` (and same-origin `/godot/` and `/browser/` routes on both servers).

- **M6 Original SVG Builders & Consent-Aware Keepsake SVG Export (`godot/main.gd`)**: Implements all 5 original SVG builders in GDScript with byte-for-byte parity against `public/*.js` (`build_keepsake_svg()` matching `public/keepsake.js` `postcardSVG()`, `build_programme_svg()` matching `public/programme.js` `programmeSVG()`, `build_tile_panel_svg()` matching `public/hosting.js` `tilePanelSVG()`, `build_civic_plan_svg()` matching `public/civic.js` `civicPlanSVG()`, and `build_film_frame_svg()` matching `public/film.js` `filmFrameSVG()`), plus `xml_escape()`. `export_keepsake_svg()` (`Shift+KEY_Y` when `keepsake_photo.status == "taken"` or `block_export_svg_btn` in `BlockPanel`) exports the consent-aware keepsake postcard (`tenderloin-redux-godot-afternoon.svg`, Rook excluded by choice, UTF-8 byte count via `TextEncoder().encode(cleanSvg).length`, and `URL.revokeObjectURL` cleanup on repeat export and `pagehide`).
- **M6 Measured Local Performance (`measure_local_performance()`)**: Benchmarks cold-cache A* pathfinding from `Thanh` (Taylor Street) to `Former Black Hawk Jazz Club` (Turk & Hyde) (`cell_walkable_cache.clear()` before `find_path()` and restored afterward, `astar_used: true`, `astar_expanded_cells > 0`, `< 250 ms`), full M1–M4 save envelope JSON serialization + parse roundtrip (`save_bytes <= 4096`, `save_roundtrip_usec < 50 ms`), and genuinely measured M5 Voices teardown (`voices_teardown_measured: true`, `voices_teardown_usec < 50 ms`), exposed to Web via `window.__tenderloinRequestPerfSnapshot()`.
- **Visual Character, Dodge Alley Block Concert & 145 Leavenworth (8th Floor) / 361 Turk 'So What' Easter Egg**:
  - **Articulated 3D Characters & Signature Neighbor Props**: Upgraded `make_person_node()` (`player` and all 5 neighbors) from simple cylinder-and-sphere pins into articulated 1.72 m figures with legs, torso coat, shoulders, arms, head, hair cap, and role-specific 3D neighbor props (`attach_neighbor_signature_prop()`: Bea's rollator walker + clipboard, Celeste's artist beret + palette, Rook's slung electric guitar + fedora, Dez's over-ear studio headphones, and Thanh's potted rosemary/herb crate), plus floating `Label3D` nameplates/signs (`make_label_3d()`) and sidewalk streetlamps/planter trees (`build_street_life_decor()`).
  - **Dodge Alley Block Concert (`StreetLifeDecor/DodgeAlleyConcert`)**: Built a 3D outdoor concert stage at the mouth of **Dodge Alley (Dodge Place)** off the north side of the 400 block of Turk Street near Hyde (`Vector3(-414.0, 0.0, 56.0)`, right beside the Black Hawk stop) with an illuminated wooden stage deck, teal backdrop arch, glowing string lights, dual PA stacks, a 3-piece live 3D combo (`ConcertHornPlayer` with brass horn, `ConcertUprightBassist` with double bass, `ConcertDrummer` with snare kit), and a floating `"Dodge Alley · Live Block Concert"` banner.
  - **Creator Easter Egg — 145 Leavenworth (8th Floor ★) & 361 Turk Street 'So What' Musical Awning (`StreetLifeDecor/SoWhatEasterEgg`)**: Built on the exact connected 2010 survey footprints `201006.0003855` (`361 Turk St`, `40.71 m`) and `201006.0001527` (`145 Leavenworth St`, `31.86 m`, sharing courtyard vertex `[-331.39, 106.56]`). Features the `SoWhatAwning_361Turk` canopy (`Vector3(-350.0, 0.0, 65.8)`) with 8 glowing brass-and-teal sculptural bars arranged after the bass walk and two-chord Dorian fourths horn voicing of Miles Davis's *"So What"* (played live at the Black Hawk down the block in 1961), a matching Leavenworth canopy (`EighthFloor_145Leavenworth` at `Vector3(-290.8, 0.0, 99.4)`), and an amber-lit **8th-floor corner bay window & juliet balcony** at `y = 24.6 m` (`EighthFloorWindow`) with a warm turntable lamp and floating `"145 Leavenworth · 8th Floor ★ ('So What')"` label. Pressing **`Shift+H`** or tapping the awning/8th-floor window calls `inspect_so_what_easter_egg()` to frame and announce the tribute.
- **Complete Cross-Track Provenance Inventory (`docs/ASSETS.md` & `tests/godot-data.test.mjs`)**: Documented all 60+ repository files in `docs/ASSETS.md` and enforced automated disk-existence and zero-network-call verification across `public/` and `godot/` in `tests/godot-data.test.mjs`.

## Historical checkpoint — Godot Round 5: Opening-block completion, browser-save adapter & mobile/accessibility validation (`2026-10-19-godot-block-validation`)

Separate `godot/` Godot 4.7.2 standard/GDScript Compatibility project now implements **Round 5 of `docs/GODOT_APPROACH.md`**: opening-block readiness/completion validation (`is_block_ready()`, `get_block_missing_summary()`, `open_block_gathering()`, 100% sound-independent completion, 3D `BlockGatheringBanner` arch and lanterns at `neighbors[0].pos + BLOCK_BANNER_OFFSET`), an explicit opt-in read-only browser v1 save adapter (`inspect_browser_save_json()`, `import_browser_save_json()` with monotonic merge, `import_browser_save_from_web_storage()`), and mobile/keyboard/nonvisual accessibility validation (`BlockPanel`, `StyleBoxFlat` focus rings on all interactive buttons, `KEY_G`/`KEY_O`/`KEY_I`, and Web DOM `aria-live="polite"` `#godot-a11y-status` + `#godot-a11y-summary` bridge). Browser 0.12.0 remains unchanged and playable at `http://127.0.0.1:4173/` (`public/` SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`); Godot Web preview runs at `http://127.0.0.1:4174/` (and same-origin `/godot/` and `/browser/` routes are served by `scripts/serve.mjs` and `scripts/serve-godot.mjs`).

Arriving at Dez or Thanh (`index >= 3`) or re-arriving at Bea (`index == 0`) opens `BlockPanel`.
- **Explicit opt-in browser v1 save adapter**: `inspect_browser_save_json()` validates `public/core.js` v1 save envelopes (`version: 1`, `core` dictionary, 2-cell horizontal/vertical furniture bounds and clear-route row `2` via `can_place_browser_furniture()`, litter IDs, mural cells/palettes, and gated `soundcheck`/`opened` flags). `import_browser_save_json()` monotonically merges valid browser v1 saves into Godot block state (unioning `visited_neighbors`, OR-ing `rook_soundcheck_done`, adopting `mural` and `palette` when `imported_mural.size() >= mural_placements.size()`, and preserving `block_opened` so importing a partial save never downgrades existing Godot progress) while leaving `tenderloin-redux.save.v1` strictly read-only. On Web, `import_browser_save_from_web_storage()` (`KEY_I` or `Import Browser Save`) reads `localStorage['tenderloin-redux.save.v1']` (shared when served via same-origin `/godot/` and `/browser/` routes) and falls back to `#tenderloin-godot-save-input` (`<input type="file" accept=".json,application/json">` + `window.__tenderloinQueueBrowserSaveJson`) when `localStorage` is empty across ports.

## Historical checkpoint — Godot Round 4: Rook soundcheck, original 80 BPM synth sketch & local Suno audition (`2026-10-18-godot-music-cues`)

Separate `godot/` Godot 4.7.2 standard/GDScript Compatibility project now implements **Round 4 of `docs/GODOT_APPROACH.md`**: **Rook's** soundcheck interaction (`"Guitar, amp & soundcheck"`, at `Vector3(114.71, 0.0, -14.79)`), a freestanding 3D **Rook's Speaker** prop (`ROOK_SPEAKER_POS = Vector3(117.10, 0.0, -15.95)`, `>= 5.5 m` clear of measured building `201006.0004056`, all 4 corners outside all 58 footprints with `>= 2.35 m` wall clearance), a sample-accurate 80 BPM 4-stem procedural synth sketch (`AudioStreamGenerator`) with progress cues, and tab-local Suno `.mp3`/`.wav`/`.ogg` audition (`docs/SUNO_MUSIC_BRIEF.md`, no Suno audio bundled). Browser 0.12.0 remains unchanged and playable at `http://127.0.0.1:4173/` (`public/` SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`); Godot Web preview runs at `http://127.0.0.1:4174/`.

- **Freestanding 3D `RookSpeaker` & Rook soundcheck**: `RookSpeaker` sidewalk monitor (`StaticBody3D` `BoxShape3D(0.86, 1.36, 0.72)`, non-walkable footprint `is_point_in_speaker_footprint()`, walkable front stand point `speaker_stand_xz()`, cached `speaker_tangent` / `speaker_normal`). Tapping `RookSpeaker`, walking to Rook (`1–5` key `3`), or pressing `KEY_R` / `"Soundcheck: Rook"` (`request_rook_soundcheck()`, walking to Rook and deferring `check_sound_with_rook()` via `pending_rook_talk` when > `2.2 m` away) opens `MusicPanel` (mutually exclusive with `MuralPanel`). `check_sound_with_rook()` sets `rook_soundcheck_done = true`, unlocks the `"brush"` and `"lead"` stems, lights the 3D `SpeakerBeacon`, and saves to the isolated Godot v1 save.
- **Explicit 80 BPM procedural synth sketch & progress cues**: `SketchAudioPlayer` (`AudioStreamGenerator`, `mix_rate = 22050.0`, `buffer_length = 0.25`) and `LocalAudioPlayer` both configure `playback_type = AudioServer.PLAYBACK_TYPE_STREAM` so Godot 4.7 Web exports mix dynamic audio without WebAudio sample-mode warnings. Never autoplays on startup or reload; requires explicit `start_music_playback()` / `toggle_music_playback()` (`KEY_K` or `Play Music` / `Pause Music`). `advance_music_clock(delta)` fills `sketch_playback.get_frames_available()` (pre-filling the `0.25 s` buffer on start), advances `sketch_frames_pushed` and `sketch_phase` strictly by generated samples, and derives `step_total`, `step_idx`, and within-step brush time `step_t` from a single sample counter (`8268.75` samples per `0.375 s` eighth-note step at 80 BPM). Synthesizes 4 stems (`"bass"` warm root + 2nd harmonic, `"brush"` deterministic snare/hi-hat burst, `"chords"` warm seventh pad, `"lead"` muted trumpet/horn phrase with 5.5 Hz vibrato) unlocked by neighbor visits, Celeste conversation/mural progress, mural completion, and Rook soundcheck. Volume cycling (`cycle_music_volume()`, `KEY_V`, `70%` -> `100%` -> `35%` -> `0%`) applies gain once via `AudioStreamPlayer.volume_db` and reports effective `last_sample_rms = last_pre_gain_rms * music_volume`.
- **Tab-local Suno MP3/WAV/OGG audition & isolated save persistence**: `load_local_music_bytes()` pre-validates extension, byte size (`<= 40 MB`), and container framing (`is_valid_pcm_wav()` for `RIFF`/`WAVE` PCM16/PCM8, `is_plausible_mp3()` for `ID3v2` + MPEG sync word, `is_plausible_ogg_vorbis()` for `OggS` + `\x01vorbis`) before decoding so corrupt/invalid files return `MUSIC_FILE_ERROR` or `MUSIC_DECODE_ERROR` cleanly without engine errors and without clobbering an active valid track. On Web exports, `setup_web_audio_bridge()` and `poll_web_audio()` use `FileReader.readAsArrayBuffer` and native Godot 4.7 `Uint8Array` -> `PackedByteArray` transfer (`window.__tenderloinQueueLocalAudioBytes`, plus `window.__tenderloinQueueLocalAudioBase64` compatibility wrapper). Local tracks hide procedural stem labels (`"Local full track (procedural stems hidden)"`), `use_synth_sketch()` (`KEY_U` or `Use Sketch`) returns to the 80 BPM sketch, and local audio is never persisted in saves. `save_godot_state()` and `load_godot_state()` persist `rook_soundcheck_done` and `visited_neighbors` (including standalone `finish_neighbor_arrival()` saves) across `user://` and Web `localStorage['tenderloin-redux.godot.save.v1']`, sorting candidates by `save_seq` so the newest valid save wins.
- **Independent CLI review & verified fixes**: One successful installed Kibitz native Claude CLI call (`--driver agy --only claude`, exact `claude-opus-5-5`, effort `high`, `rc=0`, `TOOL CHECK: PASS`, `VERDICT: yes-with-fixes`). [Raw report](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-music-cues/r3/claude.md), [scope](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-music-cues/r3/scope_receipt.md), [driver anchor](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-music-cues/r3/driver_anchor.md), [judgment](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-music-cues/r3/judgment.md), [final](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-music-cues/r3/final.md), [review summary](BUILD_GODOT_MUSIC_REVIEW.md). Confirmed and fixed all 4 Must-Fix, 9 Should-Fix, and 4 Cut items: standalone `finish_neighbor_arrival()` persistence of `visited_neighbors` + Bea-only reload test + Playwright `celeste_talk_count >= 1` wait and `visited_neighbors.includes('bea')` check; `request_rook_soundcheck()` deferral when clicking `"Soundcheck: Rook"` far from Rook; `AudioStreamGeneratorPlayback.get_frames_available()` buffer fill and single-counter sample clock; `ArrayBuffer`/`Uint8Array` -> `PackedByteArray` Web bridge; unified `finish_speaker_arrival()` / `finish_neighbor_arrival()` behavior; `is_plausible_mp3()` / `is_plausible_ogg_vorbis()` header checks + corrupt-body tests; single-stage `volume_db` scaling; `save_seq`-ordered save loading; cached street frames; and capped `cue_history`.
- **Final gates**: **94 Node tests** (`0` failures), JS syntax checks (`npm run check`), native headless Godot study/walking/collision/cutaway/mural/music/save/UI tests (`GODOT_STUDY_PASS`), negative harness sentinel (`--force-failure` exits `1` without `PASS`), official Web export, and Chrome `1100×760`, `390×667`, and `320×667` smoke tests (`rookMusicAuditionVerified: true`, `errors: []`, `external: []`, zero WebAudio sample-mode warnings, driver-inspected `godot-*-music.png` screenshots) all pass. Next bounded milestone: Godot Round 5 (block validation, explicit browser-save adapter, and mobile/accessibility checks before whole-Tenderloin migration).

## Historical checkpoint — Godot actual-scale walking, building collision, cutaway, and neighbor navigation

Separate `godot/` Godot 4.7.2 standard/GDScript Compatibility project now implements **Round 2 of `docs/GODOT_APPROACH.md`**: actual-metre walking, authoritative 2D building/courtyard-hole collision, automatic camera sightline cutaway, and keyboard/touch neighbor navigation around all 58 measured city building footprints (`DataSF ynuv-fyni` PDDL 1.0 + OpenStreetMap `ODbL 1.0` streets). Browser 0.12.0 remains unchanged and playable at `http://127.0.0.1:4173/` (`public/` SHA256 `2a9641a83499cdb6ba7f7c43d6cd9c8fa7ce34ce689b3be461b1f21ae5f84567`); Godot Web preview runs at `http://127.0.0.1:4174/`.

- **Walking & collision**: `CharacterBody3D` player (`PLAYER_HEIGHT_M = 1.72`, `PLAYER_RADIUS_M = 0.45`, `WALK_SPEED_M_PER_S = 4.8`) starting on the north Turk Street sidewalk (`17.74, -9.33`, `3.37 m` outside `201006.0006089`). Authoritative 2D polygon + courtyard-hole collision (`is_walkable_point`, `WALK_CLEARANCE_M = 0.65`, dynamic `walk_bounds` derived from `building_rects`) with sub-stepped movement (`<= 0.20 m`) and wall/street-tangent sliding (`nearest_street_tangent` derived from `data.streets`). `StaticBody3D` + `ConcavePolygonShape3D` bodies are attached to all 58 building meshes for 3D scene representation.
- **Neighbors, pathfinding & camera cutaway**: 5 neighbors (`Bea`, `Celeste`, `Rook`, `Dez`, `Thanh`) on verified sidewalks (`>= 2.35 m` tested wall clearance, actual minimum `2.39 m`). Cell-cached BFS + string-pulled sidewalk pathfinding (`find_path`) with 3D route ribbon (`route_mesh`) and continuous non-teleporting arrival (`ARRIVAL_DISTANCE_M = 1.35`, stopping `>= 0.70 m` from neighbor origin so figures never overlap). Automatic camera sightline cutaway (`update_occlusion`, shared `CAMERA_OFFSET = Vector3(180, 180, 180)`) switches occluding buildings (such as `201006.0004056` at Rook and `201006.0001275` at Thanh) to translucent `cutaway_material` (`alpha = 0.28`) without altering or moving any building geometry (`get_aabb().size.y == height_m` preserved).
- **Controls & provenance**: 6 responsive buttons (`Previous`, `Overview`, `Next building`, `Zoom in`, `Zoom out`, `Walk to neighbor`, all `>= 44 px` height in `3x2` desktop and `2x3` mobile grids), keyboard shortcuts (`WASD`/Arrows with physical keycode support, `1`–`5`, `N`/`B`/`[`/`]`, `F`, `Esc`/`X`), and 3D scene tap/drag (`> 8 px` drag threshold, single mouse/emulated-touch path). Documented in `godot/data/SOURCES.md`, `docs/GODOT_APPROACH.md`, and `docs/ROADMAP.md` why Google Open Buildings V3 (excludes North America/SF) and Google Maps Platform 3D Tiles/Street View (require live billed API key; ToS Section 3.2.3 prohibits offline scraping/caching/redistribution even for non-commercial use) are not bundled, and recorded the creator's directive that wider M2 expansion covers the **whole Tenderloin neighborhood**, not only the Transgender Cultural District.
- **Independent CLI review & verified fixes**: One successful installed Kibitz native Claude CLI call (`--driver agy --only claude`, exact `claude-opus-5-5`, effort `high`, `rc=0`, `authRc=0`, zero reviewer source edits across 99 tracked files). [Raw report](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-walking-collision/r3/claude.md), [source-verified judgment](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-walking-collision/r3/judgment.md), [scope](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-walking-collision/r3/scope_receipt.md), [final](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-walking-collision/r3/final.md), [verification](../kibitz-runs/2026-10-18-godot-walking-collision/r3/verification.json). Confirmed and fixed: removed `or current_path.is_empty()` arrival shortcut and neighbor-origin position snap so blocked routes never teleport and player/neighbor models never overlap; added continuous per-step distance and `<= 1.35 m` / `>= 0.70 m` arrival assertions in `study_test.gd`; removed duplicate `InputEventScreenTouch`/`InputEventScreenDrag` branches under Godot's default mouse emulation; gated camera follow during `advance_walk` on `following_player`; replaced hard-coded street tangents with `nearest_street_tangent` from `data.streets`; derived `walk_bounds` from `building_rects`; shared `CAMERA_OFFSET`; throttled per-tick ribbon/occlusion updates; added `_unhandled_input` tap/drag tests and `index.pck` mtime freshness guard in `tests/godot-browser.mjs`.
- **Final gates**: **94 Node tests** (`0` failures), JS syntax checks, native headless Godot study/walking/collision/cutaway/UI tests (`GODOT_STUDY_PASS`), negative harness sentinel (`--force-failure` exits `1` without `PASS`), official Web export, and Chrome `1100x760`, `390x667`, and `320x667` smoke tests (verifying overview, building selection, `Tab`+`Enter` keyboard zoom, and `Walk to neighbor` activation with zero console/page errors and zero external requests) all pass.

## Historical checkpoint — Godot measured-building foundation

Separate `godot/` Godot4.7.2 standard/GDScript Compatibility project now renders58 complete city building records around Turk/Taylor/Mason. Actual footprint geometry, multipart rings/courtyards, stableIDs and above-ground median-height metres; original neutral materials, orthographic view, per-building selection/context, responsive44px+ controls, bounded zoom and full-record overview between panels. This is a geography study, **not yet the walking/neighbor/mural/music prototype**. Browser0.12 remains unchanged and playable at4173; Godot Web study at4174. ## Historical Turk-west history walk — 0.6.0

SFJAZZ, official Miles Davis catalog and museum pages were fully read; dated source facts and the opening-year discrepancy are in SOURCES.md. ## Known limits and authoring contract

- No human playtest, measured session duration, final recorded jazz, screen-reader audit or non-Chrome compatibility certification yet.
- Real centerlines do not certify present-day entrances, facades, decorative crossings, bike-lane width or permits. Scenery is explicitly schematic. ODbL map data is separate from MIT code/original graphics; see ASSETS.md.
- Authored world coordinates assume the fixed ORIGIN, rotation and 5 pixels/metre in core.js. Do not alter that projection without updating authored scenery/props and checking routes.
- Mobile zoom/navigation and readable name labels are implemented in 0.3.0 with the evidence above. Arbitrary edge clicks can briefly snap to a nearby corridor boundary; tested quest targets remain reachable.
- In historical M0 Neighborhood Voices was absent/off. Version0.10.0 implements the default-off host/text slice and off/on/off tests above. The save envelope preserves bounded valid optional JSON independently of enabling the experiment.
- Preview runs at http://127.0.0.1:4173 in a retained local session. Reuse a healthy preview rather than starting a second server on the port; never terminate unrelated Node processes. The server serves only public/ plus a diagnostic build fingerprint, not planning/review files.
